- 2026-06-27 | agent | Session: UI unification Phase 0.1— complete the dark theme + safety-net hardening | files: `src/app/globals.css`, `src/app/layout.tsx`, `scripts/check-contrast.mjs`, `scripts/contrast-baseline.json`, `playwright.config.ts`, `tests/visual/screenshots.pw.ts-snapshots/*`, `tests/visual/a11y-baseline.json` | Summary: Started Phase 0 of the UI unification plan and, while double-checking Phase 0a, found and fixed **two real safety-net bugs**. (1) **Contrast script bug:** `extractBlock('.dark')` matched the `@custom-variant dark (&:where(...))` mention on line 4, not the real `.dark { }` rule, so it silently compared dark→light (all 52 "passed" identically). Fixed extractor to require a `selector` immediately followed by `{`. The corrected script then exposed **10 genuine dark-mode contrast failures** (title 2.35:1, status/power/martial/health text on dark tinted bgs, white-on-light primary). (2) **Stale-server bug:** Playwright `webServer.reuseExistingServer: true` reused a long-running server from an earlier build, so visual baselines + a11y scans were validated against the wrong build (default `#9e9eff` links on white = CSS not applied). Set `reuseExistingServer: false` so the suite always serves the build under test. **Token work (Phase 0.1):** added theme-aware semantic foreground tokens (`--color-success-fg`/`-danger-fg`/`-warning-fg`/`-info-fg`/`-power-fg`/`-martial-fg`, `--color-primary-button`) and filled every previously-light-only dark token (`title`, `health-text`, all 7 `category-*` chips, `ally/enemy/companion`, `accent-chip/-light`, `card`, `muted`, `muted-foreground`, `destructive`). Light mode unchanged (contrast ratios identical). Contrast now **56/56 pass in BOTH themes (real)**, baseline locked at 0. **Determinism fix:** self-hosted `Nova Flat` via `next/font` (was a runtime Google-Fonts CDN `<link>` with `display=swap` → layout reflow— flaky baselines); wired `--font-display` to `var(--font-nova-flat)`. Regenerated 54 visual baselines + re-recorded a11y baseline against correct fresh servers; full `npm run verify` green (contrast/lint/visual 54/visual a11y 30). Build exit 0. **Workflow hardening:** `verify` + `verify:visual:update` + `verify:a11y:update` now `npm run build` first (stale `.next` was the deeper foot-gun beyond stale running servers); added durable roadmap `src/docs/ai/UI_UNIFICATION_PLAN.md` (the plan was a Cursor plan-mode artifact never persisted— now source-controlled and referenced from `AGENT_GUIDE.md` + `AGENTS.md` source-of-truth map); documented token architecture (theme-aware `*-fg` tokens) + hard-won gotchas (build-first, `reuseExistingServer:false`, self-hosted fonts, "verify your verifier"; `#9e9eff`-links-on-white = stale/unstyled render tell). Remaining Phase 0: dual-namespace collapse (legacy `:root` → `var(--color-*)` aliases), standard ladders (radius/elevation/motion/focus/containers) as tokens, prune verified-dead CSS, rewrite `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md`.
